<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 116J. EMPLOYMENT AND ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT</h2>
      
      <h1 class="fgh">GENERAL</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.116J.01">
        <h1 class="shn">116J.01 DEPARTMENT OF EMPLOYMENT AND ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.01.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Appointment.</span></h2>
          <p>The Department of Employment and Economic Development is supervised and controlled by the commissioner of employment and economic development, who is appointed by the governor and serves under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.06">15.06</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.01.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Confidential secretary.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may appoint a confidential secretary in the unclassified service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.01.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/163/#laws.1.35.0">1993 c 163 art 1 s 35</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.01.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/171/#laws.0.20.0">2004 c 171 s 20</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.01.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Departmental organization.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall organize the department as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.06">15.06</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may establish divisions and offices within the department. The commissioner may employ four deputy commissioners in the unclassified service.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner shall:</p>
          <p>(1) employ assistants and other officers, employees, and agents that the commissioner considers necessary to discharge the functions of the commissioner's office;</p>
          <p>(2) define the duties of the officers, employees, and agents, and delegate to them any of the commissioner's powers, duties, and responsibilities, subject to the commissioner's control and under conditions prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(d) The commissioner shall ensure that there are at least three employment and economic development officers in state offices in nonmetropolitan areas of the state who will work with local units of government on developing local employment and economic development.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.01.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Unclassified positions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may establish positions in the unclassified service in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.08">43A.08</a>. The commissioner may appoint and define the duties of other subordinate officers and employees as the commissioner deems necessary to discharge the functions of the department.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may establish the position of director of the state job training office in the unclassified service.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.116J.01.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/356/#laws.0.64.0">1981 c 356 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/289/#laws.0.40.0">1983 c 289 s 40</a>-42; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/558/#laws.4.3.0">1984 c 558 art 4 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.9.39.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 9 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/312/#laws.1.14.0">1987 c 312 art 1 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.136.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 136</a>,137; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/571/#laws.0.42.0">1990 c 571 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/589/#laws.1.2.0">1990 c 589 art 1 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/261/#laws.0.1.0">1991 c 261 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/163/#laws.1.12.0">1993 c 163 art 1 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/483/#laws.0.1.0">1994 c 483 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/369/#laws.0.2.0">1996 c 369 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/200/#laws.1.50.0">1997 c 200 art 1 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/175/#laws.0.52.0">2001 c 175 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/200/#laws.0.1.0">2001 c 200 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/4/#laws.0.1.0">1Sp2003 c 4 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/171/#laws.0.3.0">2004 c 171 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/206/#laws.0.8.0">2004 c 206 s 8,9</a>,52; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/13/#laws.2.15.0">2007 c 13 art 2 s 15</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.116J.011">
        <h1 class="shn">116J.011 MISSION.</h1>
        <p>The mission of the Department of Employment and Economic Development is to facilitate an economic environment that produces net new job growth in excess of the national average while improving the quality of the state workforce. These actions will support the economic success of Minnesota individuals, businesses, and communities by providing opportunities for growth. It is part of the department's mission that within the department's resources the commissioner shall endeavor to:</p>
        <p>(1) prevent the waste or unnecessary spending of public money;</p>
        <p>(2) use innovative fiscal and human resource practices to manage the state's resources and operate the department as efficiently as possible;</p>
        <p>(3) coordinate the department's activities wherever appropriate with the activities of other governmental agencies;</p>
        <p>(4) use technology where appropriate to increase agency productivity, improve customer service, increase public access to information about government, and increase public participation in the business of government;</p>
        <p>(5) utilize constructive and cooperative labor-management practices to the extent otherwise required by chapters 43A and 179A;</p>
        <p>(6) report to the legislature on the performance of agency operations and the accomplishment of agency goals in the agency's biennial budget according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.10#stat.16A.10.1">16A.10, subdivision 1</a>; and</p>
        <p>(7) recommend to the legislature appropriate changes in law necessary to carry out the mission and improve the performance of the department.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.116J.011.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/163/#laws.1.13.0">1993 c 163 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/248/#laws.11.8.0">1995 c 248 art 11 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/366/#laws.0.55.0">1998 c 366 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.13.5.0">2003 c 128 art 13 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/4/#laws.0.1.0">1Sp2003 c 4 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/206/#laws.0.10.0">2004 c 206 s 10</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.116J.0124">
        <h1 class="shn">116J.0124 PLAIN LANGUAGE IN WRITTEN MATERIALS.</h1>
        <p>(a) To the extent reasonable and consistent with the goals of providing easily understandable and readable materials and complying with federal and state laws governing the programs, all written materials relating to services and determinations of eligibility for or amounts of benefits that will be given to applicants for or recipients of assistance under a program administered or supervised by the commissioner of employment and economic development must be understandable to a person of average intelligence and education.</p>
        <p>(b) All written materials relating to determinations of eligibility for or amounts of benefits that will be given to applicants for or recipients of assistance under programs administered or supervised by the commissioner of employment and economic development must be developed to satisfy the plain language requirements of the Plain Language Contract Act under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325G.29">325G.29</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325G.36">325G.36</a>. Materials may be submitted to the attorney general for review and certification. Regardless of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325G.35#stat.325G.35.1">325G.35, subdivision 1</a>, the attorney general shall review submitted materials to determine whether they comply with the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325G.31">325G.31</a>. The remedies available pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/8.31">8.31</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325G.33">325G.33</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325G.36">325G.36</a> do not apply to these materials. Failure to comply with this section does not provide a basis for suspending the implementation or operation of other laws governing programs administered by the commissioner.</p>
        <p>(c) The requirements of this section apply to all materials modified or developed by the commissioner on or after July 1, 1988. The requirements of this section do not apply to materials that must be submitted to a federal agency for approval, to the extent that application of the requirements prevents federal approval.</p>
        <p>(d) Nothing in this section may be construed to prohibit a lawsuit brought to require the commissioner to comply with this section.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.116J.0124.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/689/#laws.2.220.0">1988 c 689 art 2 s 220</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/483/#laws.0.1.0">1994 c 483 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/259/#laws.1.41.0">1995 c 259 art 1 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/339/#laws.0.4.0">1996 c 339 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/7/#laws.1.104.0">1997 c 7 art 1 s 104</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/175/#laws.0.52.0">2001 c 175 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/206/#laws.0.52.0">2004 c 206 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/86/#laws.1.89.0">2009 c 86 art 1 s 89</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.116J.0125">
        <h1 class="shn">116J.0125 ANNUAL REPORT TO LEGISLATURE.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner shall provide to the legislature no later than January 15 of each year a report of department programs and services. The report must include:</p>
        <p>(1) a description of the department's programs and services;</p>
        <p>(2) the number of clients served by each program or service;</p>
        <p>(3) an evaluation of each program or service; and</p>
        <p>(4) recommendations for changes or improvements to the programs or services.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.116J.0125.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/339/#laws.0.5.0">1996 c 339 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/206/#laws.0.52.0">2004 c 206 s 52</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.116J.013">
        <h1 class="shn">116J.013 COST-OF-LIVING STUDY; ANNUAL REPORT.</h1>
        <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct an annual cost-of-living study in Minnesota. The study shall include:</p>
        <p>(1) a calculation of the statewide basic needs cost of living, adjusted for family size;</p>
        <p>(2) a calculation of the basic needs cost of living, adjusted for family size, for each county;</p>
        <p>(3) an analysis of statewide and county cost-of-living data, employment data, and job vacancy data; and</p>
        <p>(4) recommendations to aid in the assessment of employment and economic development planning needs throughout the state.</p>
        <p>(b) The commissioner shall report on the cost-of-living study and recommendations by February 1 of each year to the governor and to the chairs of the standing committees of the house of representatives and the senate having jurisdiction over employment and economic development issues.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.116J.013.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/85/#laws.3.1.0">2013 c 85 art 3 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.116J.014">
        <h1 class="shn">116J.014 COOPERATION WITH OTHER STATE AGENCIES.</h1>
        <p>To effectively coordinate job training and placement services with future job needs of the state the commissioner shall maintain close liaison, coordination and cooperation with the commissioner of any other state agency involved in employment issues affecting the state.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.116J.014.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/430/#laws.0.13.0">1977 c 430 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/356/#laws.0.186.0">1981 c 356 s 186</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/289/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 289 s 115</a> subd 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/312/#laws.1.26.0">1987 c 312 art 1 s 26</a> subd 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/4/#laws.0.1.0">1Sp2003 c 4 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/206/#laws.0.41.0">2004 c 206 s 41</a>,52</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.116J.015">
        <h1 class="shn">116J.015 LEGISLATIVE REVIEW; EXPIRATION OF REPORT MANDATES.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner shall submit to the chairs and ranking minority members of the legislative committees with jurisdiction over employment and economic development by February 15 of each year, beginning February 15, 2024, the following:</p>
        <p>(1) a list of all reports required from the commissioner of employment and economic development to the legislature that are mandated by statute and the enabling legislation does not include a date for the submission of a final report;</p>
        <p>(2) a discussion regarding the usefulness of continuation for each report; and</p>
        <p>(3) any additional information the commissioner considers appropriate regarding whether the reporting requirement should be set to expire by the legislature.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.116J.015.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/33/#laws.0.1.0">2023 c 33 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/73/#laws.0.1.0">2023 c 73 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.116J.02"><b>116J.02</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/289/#laws.0.119.0">1983 c 289 s 119</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.116J.03">
        <h1 class="shn">116J.03 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.03.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>As used in this chapter, the terms defined in this section have the meaning given them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.03.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commissioner" means the commissioner of employment and economic development.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.03.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Department.</span></h2>
          <p>"Department" means the Department of Employment and Economic Development.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.116J.03.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/356/#laws.0.66.0">1981 c 356 s 66</a>,248; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/289/#laws.0.43.0">1983 c 289 s 43</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/312/#laws.1.15.0">1987 c 312 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/4/#laws.0.1.0">1Sp2003 c 4 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.116J.035">
        <h1 class="shn">116J.035 POWERS OF COMMISSIONER; RULES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.035.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Powers.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may:</p>
          <p>(1) apply for, receive, and expend money from municipal, county, regional, and other government agencies;</p>
          <p>(2) apply for, accept, and disburse grants and other aids from other public or private sources;</p>
          <p>(3) contract for professional services if such work or services cannot be satisfactorily performed by employees of the department or by any other state agency;</p>
          <p>(4) enter into interstate compacts to jointly carry out such research and planning with other states or the federal government where appropriate;</p>
          <p>(5) distribute informational material at no cost to the public upon reasonable request; and</p>
          <p>(6) enter into contracts necessary for the performance of the commissioner's duties with federal, state, regional, metropolitan, local, and other agencies or units of government; educational institutions, including the University of Minnesota. Contracts made pursuant to this section shall not be subject to the competitive bidding requirements of chapter 16C.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may apply for, receive, and expend money made available from federal or other sources for the purpose of carrying out the duties and responsibilities of the commissioner pursuant to this chapter.</p>
          <p>(c) All moneys received by the commissioner pursuant to this chapter shall be deposited in the state treasury and, subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.3005">3.3005</a>, are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose for which the moneys have been received. The money shall not cancel and shall be available until expended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.035.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Promotional contracts.</span></h2>
          <p>In order to best carry out duties and responsibilities and to serve the people of the state in the promotion of tourism, trade, and economic development, the commissioner may engage in programs and projects jointly with a private person, firm, corporation or association and may enter into contracts under terms to be mutually agreed upon to carry out such programs and projects not including acquisition of land or buildings. Contracts may be negotiated and are not subject to the provisions of chapter 16C relating to competitive bidding.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.035.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Rules.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may adopt rules pursuant to chapter 14 as necessary to carry out the commissioner's duties and responsibilities.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.035.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/403/#laws.2.164.0">1987 c 403 art 2 s 164</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.035.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Delegation of powers.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may delegate, in written orders filed with the secretary of state, any powers or duties subject to the commissioner's control to officers and employees in the department. Regardless of any other law, the commissioner may delegate the execution of specific contracts or specific types of contracts to the commissioner's deputies, an assistant commissioner, or a program director if the delegation has been approved by the commissioner of administration and filed with the secretary of state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.035.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Demonstration projects.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may conduct and administer demonstration projects to test methods and procedures for providing employment and training services. The demonstration must provide new methods and procedures of administration and must not conflict with the basic purposes, coverage, or benefits provided by law. No demonstration project authorized by this section is effective until any required approval by a federal agency is obtained and the comprehensive plan, including the estimated project costs, is filed with the commissioner of administration.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.035.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Receipt of gifts, money; appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may:</p>
          <p>(1) apply for, accept, and disburse gifts, bequests, grants, payments for services, loans, or other property from the United States, the state, private foundations, or any other source;</p>
          <p>(2) enter into an agreement required for the gifts, grants, or loans; and</p>
          <p>(3) hold, use, and dispose of its assets according to the terms of the gift, grant, loan, or agreement.</p>
          <p>(b) Money received by the commissioner under this subdivision must be deposited in a separate account in the state treasury and invested by the State Board of Investment. The amount deposited, including investment earnings, is appropriated to the commissioner to carry out duties under this section.</p>
          <p>(c) Money received by the commissioner under this subdivision for State Services for the Blind is exempt from depositing gifts, bequests, charitable contributions, and similar contributions made solely into the state treasury.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.035.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Monitoring pass-through grant recipients.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall monitor the activities and outcomes of programs and services funded by legislative appropriations and administered by the department on a pass-through basis. Unless amounts are otherwise appropriated for administrative costs, the commissioner may retain up to five percent of the amount appropriated to the department for grants to pass-through entities. Amounts retained are deposited to a special revenue account and are appropriated to the commissioner for costs incurred in administering and monitoring the pass-through grants.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.116J.035.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Minnesota Business First Stop.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner of employment and economic development shall, through the multiagency collaboration called "Minnesota Business First Stop," ensure the coordination, development, implementation, and administration of state permits, including:</p>
          <p>(1) establishing a mechanism in state government that will coordinate administrative decision-making procedures and related quasijudicial and judicial review pertaining to permits related to the state's air, land, and water resources;</p>
          <p>(2) providing coordination and understanding between federal, state, and local governmental units in the administration of the various programs relating to air, water, and land resources;</p>
          <p>(3) identifying all existing state permits, reviews, and other approvals, compliance schedules, or other programs that pertain to the use of natural resources and protection of the environment; and</p>
          <p>(4) recommending legislative or administrative modifications to existing permit programs to increase their efficiency and utility.</p>
          <p>(b) A person proposing a project may apply to Minnesota Business First Stop for assistance in obtaining necessary state permits, reviews, and other approvals. Upon request, the commissioner shall to the extent practicable:</p>
          <p>(1) provide a list of all federal, state, and local permits and other required reviews and approvals for the project;</p>
          <p>(2) provide a plan that will coordinate federal, state, and local administrative decision-making practices, including monitoring, analysis and reporting, public comments and hearings, and issuances of permits and approvals;</p>
          <p>(3) provide a timeline for the issuance of all federal, state, and local permits and other reviews and approvals required for the project;</p>
          <p>(4) coordinate the execution of any memorandum of understanding between the person proposing a project and any federal, state, or local agency;</p>
          <p>(5) coordinate all federal, state, or local public comment periods and hearings; and</p>
          <p>(6) provide other assistance requested to facilitate final approval and issuance of all federal, state, and local permits and other approvals required for the project.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.1283">16A.1283</a>, as necessary, the commissioner may negotiate a schedule to assess the project proposer for reasonable costs that any state agency incurs in the coordination, development, implementation, and administration of state permits, and the proposer shall pay the assessed costs to the commissioner. Money received by the commissioner must be credited to an account in the special revenue fund and is appropriated to the commissioner to cover the assessed costs incurred.</p>
          <p>(d) Reimbursement by a project proposer shall precede and not be contingent upon issuance of a permit and shall not affect any state agency's decision on whether to issue or deny a permit, what conditions are included in a permit, or the application of state and federal statutes and rules governing permit determinations.</p>
          <p>(e) The coordination of the development, implementation, and administration of state permits is not governmental action under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116D.04">116D.04</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.116J.035.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1947/0/587/#laws.0.6.0">1947 c 587 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/299/#laws.0.5.0">1967 c 299 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/356/#laws.0.248.0">1981 c 356 s 248</a>;
